Because the benchmark is a generator rather than a fixed dataset, it has properties no fixed dataset can match: an unlimited supply of fresh problems, a difficulty knob set by $(n,m)$, ground-truth answers correct by construction, low LLM-side cost per problem relative to human authoring, repeatable scores across independent batches, and resistance to training-data leakage when fresh post-cutoff seed ranges are used.

</details>


### [250] [A rubric-based controlled comparison of frontier language models on expert-authored clinical reasoning tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02175)
*Samiha A. Ismail,Fan X. Chen,Ali Merali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对开放式临床性能评估不足，提出一个小型高难度数据集（5个临床场景、184个加权标准），发现前沿LLMs在关键医疗标准上表现极差（通过率32.4-41.7%），而在低风险标准上表现良好（80-90%），并验证了LLM自动评分器可靠性（重现率92.8-94.7%），为大规模基准开发提供可扩展方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项选择题医疗基准已趋饱和，但开放式临床性能评估（如HealthBench）远未解决，其"Hard"子集顶分仅32%，亟需高难度评估工具以揭示模型真实临床能力瓶颈。

Method: 构建由5个临床医生撰写的临床场景（涵盖麻醉、内科/家庭医学、急诊医学、妇产科），每个场景配原子化加权MECE评分标准（25-62个标准/任务，总计184个）；评估GPT 5.4、Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro三个模型；采用LLM自动评分器验证专家标签一致性。

Result: 平均评分通过率：Claude 0.47、GPT 0.39、Gemini 0.37；核心发现为临床优先级反转——高权重（权重-5，关键）标准通过率仅32.4-41.7%，而低权重（权重-1）标准达80-90%；108个关键标准中56个（52%）无任何模型满足；LLM自动评分器在552个标准上重现专家标签率92.8-94.7%。

Conclusion: 作为方法与初步发现贡献，该数据集证明了可扩展、可防御的评估管道可行性，为构建大规模临床性能基准奠定基础，并突显了LLMs在关键医疗决策中的重大缺陷。

Abstract: Multiple-choice medical benchmarks are increasingly saturated, and recent rubric-based evaluations such as HealthBench have shown that open-ended clinical performance is far from solved - its "Hard" subset top score remains 32%. We present a small, deliberately difficult evaluation dataset of five clinician-authored clinical scenarios spanning four specialties (anaesthesia, internal/family medicine, emergency medicine, and obstetrics), each accompanied by an atomic, weighted, MECE rubric (25-62 criteria per task; 184 criteria total) authored from a clinician-drafted golden answer. We evaluate three frontier models: GPT 5.4, Claude Opus 4.7, and Gemini 3.1 Pro. Mean rubric pass rates were 0.47 (Claude), 0.39 (GPT), and 0.37 (Gemini). The central finding is an inversion of clinical priority: the highest-weighted (weight-5, critical) criteria passed at only 32.4-41.7%, while low-stakes weight-1 criteria passed at 80-90%. 56 of 108 critical (weight-5) criteria (52%) were satisfied by no model. Three LLM autoraters reproduced expert met/not-met labels on 92.8-94.7% of 552 graded criteria. We position this as a methods-and-preliminary-findings contribution: the five tasks demonstrate a scalable, defensible pipeline ready to develop into a large-scale benchmark.

</details>


### [251] [AgenticSTS: A Bounded-Memory Testbed for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02255)
*Xiangchen Cheng,Yunwei Jiang,Jianwen Sun,Zizhen Li,Chuanhao Li,Xiangcheng Cao,Yihao Liu,Fanrui Zhang,Li Jin,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种新型长序列LLM智能体记忆框架：每次决策仅基于类型检索生成的新消息，避免传统方法中混杂的跨决策上下文。在卡牌游戏《杀戮尖塔2》验证，显示显式策略记忆层可提升胜率（3→6胜/10局），虽统计不显著但提供可复现研究平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长序列LLM智能体将全部历史对话附加至每次提示，导致上下文混杂且无法隔离单个记忆模块的影响，阻碍了对决策机制的精确分析。

Method: 设计有界记忆契约：每次决策仅使用类型检索生成的新消息，不附加原始跨决策记录；在《杀戮尖塔2》中实现并测试，通过消融实验比较基础模型与添加策略技能层的性能差异。

Result: 基础模型在低难度获胜率30%（3/10），加入策略层后达60%（6/10）；Fisher检验p≈0.37表明方向性效果但统计不显著；发布包含298条轨迹的开源测试平台，含条件标签和完整日志。

Conclusion: 提出可复现的研究框架，证明显式分层记忆能改善长序列决策，为LLM智能体设计提供标准化方法论。

Abstract: Memory for a long-horizon LLM agent is a contract about what each future decision is allowed to see. The simplest contract appends past observations, tool calls, and reflections to every prompt, which makes prior context easy to access but also turns it into a jumbled mixture in which the effect of any single memory component is hard to isolate. We introduce and instrument an alternative bounded contract: every decision is made from a fresh user message assembled by typed retrieval, with no raw cross-decision transcript appended. The prompt thus stays bounded across runs of any length, and any single layer can be ablated in isolation. We instantiate the contract in Slay the Spire 2, a closed-rule stochastic deck-building game whose runs require hundreds of tactical and strategic decisions. A public online benchmark of frontier LLMs on the same game reports zero wins at the lowest difficulty across five configurations, and the developer-reported human win rate at the same difficulty is 16%; the task is hard but not saturated. Within our harness, a fixed-A0 ablation shows the largest observed difference when triggered strategic skills are enabled: the no-store baseline wins 3/10 games and adding the skill layer 6/10. At this sample size the comparison is directional rather than statistically decisive (Fisher exact p\approx0.37); a cross-backbone probe and public accumulating-context baselines are reported as operational comparisons rather than controlled tests of the contract variable itself. We release a reproducible testbed: 298 completed trajectories with condition tags, frozen memory/skill snapshots, prompt records, and analysis scripts -- an agent design and a validated, reusable methodology for studying how explicit memory layers shape long-horizon LLM-agent decisions.

</details>


### [252] [UA-ChatDev: Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Agent Collaboration for Reliable Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02186)
*Temitayo Olamilekan Ogunsusi,Lijun Qian,Xishuang Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UA-ChatDev是一个不确定性感知的多代理软件开发框架，通过集成不确定性量化和基于检索的验证来解决幻觉传播问题，显著提升了软件质量和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多代理软件开发框架通常假设中间代理输出同样可靠，导致幻觉传播风险：早期阶段的错误决策会传递到下游代理，损害最终软件质量。

Method: 提出UA-ChatDev框架，包含轻量级不确定性估计机制（基于token-level log probabilities）评估代理响应置信度，并采用阶段感知阈值校准，当不确定性超过阈值时选择性触发基于检索的验证。

Result: 在SRDD基准上，UA-ChatDev在完整性、可执行性、一致性和整体质量指标上全面优于现有单代理和多代理框架；消融研究和通信分析验证了不确定性感知交互提升了代码执行可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性感知交互，UA-ChatDev有效缓解幻觉传播问题，证明了其在多代理软件开发中的优越性和可靠性提升效果。

Abstract: Software development is a complex task that demands cooperation among agents with diverse roles. Large language models (LLMs) have enabled autonomous multi-agent software development frameworks that leverage role-based collaboration to automate requirements analysis, coding, testing, and refinement. However, existing approaches typically assume that intermediate agent outputs are equally reliable, leaving them vulnerable to hallucination propagation, where incorrect decisions generated in early development phases are transferred to downstream agents and negatively impact final software quality. To address this challenge, we propose UA-ChatDev, an uncertainty-aware multi-agent software development framework that integrates uncertainty quantification into agent interactions. It introduces a lightweight uncertainty estimation mechanism based on token-level log probabilities to assess the confidence of agent responses and employs phase-aware threshold calibration to selectively trigger retrieval-based verification when uncertainty exceeds acceptable levels. Extensive experiments on the SRDD benchmark demonstrate that UA-ChatDev consistently outperforms existing single-agent and multi-agent software development frameworks across completeness, executability, consistency, and overall quality metrics. Further ablation studies and communication analyses verify that uncertainty-aware interactions enhance code execution reliability.

</details>


### [253] [Automated grading of Linux/bash examinations using large language models: a four-level cognitive taxonomy approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02432)
*Manuel Alonso-Carracedo,Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,Pedro Celard,Francisco J. Rodriguez-Martinez,Lorena Otero-Cerdeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现大语言模型在命令行考试评分中表现良好，尤其是Gemini 3.0 Pro在增强提示下达最高一致性，但随问题复杂度增加一致性下降；评分标准质量比模型选择更重要，并提出了一个框架指导AI评分适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展和可靠的命令行考试评分是计算机教育的挑战，因注册人数增加使人工评分困难，而基于规则的自动评分器无法处理部分信用、等效解决方案或语法变化。

Method: 评估四个前沿大语言模型（GPT、Claude Opus、Gemini、GLM）在评分短Linux/bash命令响应时能否近似专家判断，采用四级认知分类法（L1信息检索至L4高级系统管理），模型在两种提示变体（最小基线和增强版）下对1200个真实学生响应进行测试，由三位专家教师独立评分。

Result: Gemini 3.0 Pro与基于评分标准的提示实现最高人机一致性（ICC(3,1)=0.888, MAE=0.10），一致性随分类法级别增加而下降，高级别差异最大；评分标准质量比模型选择影响更大，结构化提示持续改善一致性。

Conclusion: 问题复杂度是LLMs准确评分难度的可靠预测因子，建立了基于分类法的框架用于确定哪些问题适合AI辅助评分、哪些需人工审查，并提供了可转移的评估协议和提示模板。

Abstract: Scalable and reliable grading of command-line examinations remains a challenge in computing education, where rising enrolments make manual marking difficult and rule-based autograders cannot handle partial credit, equivalent solutions, or syntactic variation. This paper evaluates whether four frontier Large Language Models (GPT, Claude Opus, Gemini, and GLM) can approximate expert judgment when grading short Linux/bash command responses. The study adopts a four-level cognitive taxonomy that combines cognitive complexity and operational impact, ranging from information retrieval (L1) and basic file manipulation (L2) to structural operations (L3) and advanced system management (L4). The models were tested with two prompt variants, a minimal baseline and a rubric-enhanced version, on 1200 real responses from second-year Computer Engineering students independently graded by three expert instructors. Gemini~3.0 Pro with rubric-guided prompting achieved the highest human-AI agreement (ICC(3,1) = 0.888, MAE = 0.10, Bland-Altman bias = -0.014). Agreement declined consistently as taxonomy level increased, with the largest discrepancies at higher levels. Across all models, rubric quality had a larger effect than provider choice, with structured prompts consistently improving agreement. These results show that question complexity is a reliable predictor of the difficulty LLMs face in grading accurately, and they establish a principled, taxonomy-based framework for determining which questions are suitable for AI-assisted grading and which require human review, while also providing a transferable evaluation protocol and prompt templates.

</details>


### [254] [EvoPolicyGym: Evaluating Autonomous Policy Evolution in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02440)
*Zhilin Wang,Han Song,Runzhe Zhan,Jusen Du,Jiacheng Chen,Tianle Li,Qingyu Yin,Yulun Wu,Zhennan Shen,Tong Zhu,Yanshu Li,Guanjie Chen,Derek F. Wong,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了自主策略进化(Autonomous Policy Evolution)评估框架和EvoPolicyGym基准，用于评估AI代理在有限交互预算下迭代改进可执行策略的能力。GPT-5.5在该基准上表现最佳，并发现成功的策略进化不仅需要任务性能，还需要发现合适的优化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法往往将自主代理通过反馈改进策略的复杂过程简化为最终得分，或将其与开放式软件工程进展混淆，缺乏对策略迭代优化过程的精细化评估。需要一种受控的评估设置来专门测量代理在有限交互预算下重复编辑和改进可执行策略系统的能力。

Method: 提出自主策略进化评估框架，创建EvoPolicyGym基准，该基准基于紧凑的交互式强化学习环境，评估代理如何在固定交互预算内反复编辑可执行策略系统。该框架提供轨迹级诊断，区分代理如何分配预算、将反馈转化为参数调优。

Result: 在EvoPolicyGym套件上，GPT-5.5取得了最高的聚合排名分数，并在所有16个环境中都实现了前二名性能。轨迹级分析揭示了代理在预算分配和反馈转化方面的不同策略。

Conclusion: 强大的自主策略进化不仅依赖于孤立的任务胜利，更重要的是发现任务适当的机制并在有界反馈约束下持续完善策略。该基准不仅提供排行榜结果，还提供详细的诊断分析，帮助理解代理策略改进的机制。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly expected to improve executable policies through feedback, yet existing evaluations often collapse this process into a final score or confound it with open-ended software-engineering progress. We introduce Autonomous Policy Evolution, a controlled evaluation setting in which a harness-model agent repeatedly edits an executable policy system under a fixed interaction budget. We instantiate this setting in EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark built from compact interactive RL environments that evaluates how agents iteratively improve explored policies. On the EvoPolicyGym suite, GPT-5.5 achieves the strongest aggregate rank score and top-two performance on all 16 environments. Beyond leaderboard results, EvoPolicyGym also provides trajectory-level diagnostics that distinguish how agents allocate budget, convert feedback into parametric tuning. These analyses show that strong autonomous policy evolution depends not only on isolated task wins, but on discovering task-appropriate mechanisms and refining policies under bounded feedback.

</details>


### [255] [Purified OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation Without Losing How to Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02234)
*Zhanming Shen,Jintao Tong,Shaotian Yan,Chen Shen,Hao Chen,Wentao Ye,Xiaomeng Hu,Rui Miao,Haobo Wang,Junbo Zhao,Gang Chen,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPSD在长链推理模型上失败，作者通过监督信号分解和PMI机制提出解决方案，在四个模型两个数据集上实现稳定改进，同时保留模型反思能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD在长链推理模型上持续失效，仅能获得边际改进且破坏模型依赖的反思推理能力，需要找出根本原因并解决这一问题。

Method: 通过教师监督信号分解，发现参考诱导部分主导监督导致死记硬背特定捷径；提出两步解决方案：1)构建参考答案条件化教师模型隔离非可转移部分，获得问题条件化可转移修正；2)使用点互信息(PMI)将残余转换为可蒸馏的目标分布。

Result: 在四个长链推理模型和两个数据集上实验，相比基础模型和标准OPSD都取得持续改进，同时保持模型的天然认识论行为。

Conclusion: 解决了OPSD在长链推理场景下的失效问题，提出了有效的监督信号分解和PMI转换机制，实现了稳定改进而不损害模型反思能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving LLM reasoning, where a privileged teacher with access to reference solutions provides token-level supervision on the student's own generated trajectories. However, we find that OPSD consistently fails on long chain-of-thought (long-CoT) reasoning models, yielding at best marginal gains while destabilizing the reflective reasoning capability these models depend on. Through a novel decomposition of the teacher's supervision signal, we identify the root cause: the teacher's supervision is dominated by a reference-induced component that drives rote memorization of reference-specific shortcuts, while the question-conditioned, inference-transferable component is ignored or actively opposed. Based on this diagnosis, we propose a two-step solution. First, we construct a reference-only teacher (the same model conditioned on the reference without the question) to isolate the non-transferable component of the supervision signal; the residual after subtracting this component captures the question-conditioned, inference-transferable correction. Second, we use pointwise mutual information (PMI) as the mechanism to transform this residual into a well-formed PMI target distribution that the student can directly distill from, filtering out the reference-induced shortcut. Experiments on four long-CoT models across two datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over both the base model and standard OPSD, while preserving the models' natural epistemic behavior throughout training.

</details>


### [256] [Copewell: A Multi-Agent Swarm Architecture for Equitable Mental Wellness Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02245)
*Seren Yenikent,Jack Vinijtrongjit,Katherine Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Copewell是一个多智能体AI系统，通过整合多源数据、情绪映射和双模式干预，为精神健康支持提供可及、个性化的解决方案，同时内置隐私保护和伦理监督机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球近10亿人受精神健康障碍影响，但低收入和中等收入国家75%的人得不到治疗（人力短缺、成本障碍、污名化）。现有AI健康方案依赖单一对话界面，放弃率高，无法提供可测量的即时缓解，也无法适应用户动态情感状态。

Method: 1. 多源评估框架：整合自我报告、生理和情境数据，减少算法偏见；2. 效价-唤醒情绪映射：基于Russell情感环状模型，将用户路由到专门AI代理；3. 双模式干预：结合对话支持和循证感官健康协议。系统采用隐私优先架构、Ethics Supervisor代理进行伦理监督、精神健康从业者参与的共同设计。

Result: 早期从业者参与和Beta部署为设计决策提供了依据，并识别了未来实证评估的方向。系统成功将公平和安全原则从设计之初就嵌入技术架构中。

Conclusion: Copewell展示了技术架构如何从源头实现和运作公平与安全原则，为负责任的AI讨论做出了贡献，证明了多智能体系统在扩大精神健康支持可及性方面的潜力。

Abstract: Mental health disorders affect nearly one billion people globally, yet 75% of individuals in low- and middle-income countries receive no treatment due to workforce shortages, cost barriers, and stigma. Current AI-powered wellness solutions predominantly rely on single-mode conversational interfaces that suffer high abandonment rates and fail to provide measurable, immediate relief calibrated to users' dynamic emotional states. This paper presents Copewell, a novel multi-agent swarm system designed to expand access to mental wellness support through human-centered AI principles. Our architecture introduces three technical innovations: (1) a multi-source assessment framework integrating self-reported, physiological, and contextual data to mitigate algorithmic bias; (2) valence-arousal emotion mapping using Russell's Circumplex Model of Affect to route users to specialized AI agents; and (3) dual-mode intervention delivery combining conversational support with evidence-based sensory wellness protocols. We examine the sociotechnical design considerations underlying Copewell's development, including a privacy-first architecture, embedded ethical oversight through a dedicated Ethics Supervisor agent, and participatory design informed by mental health practitioners. Early practitioner engagement and beta deployment inform design decisions and identify directions for future empirical evaluation. This work contributes to responsible AI discourse by demonstrating how technical architecture can operationalize equity and safety principles from inception.

</details>


### [257] [What LLM Agents Say When No One Is Watching: Social Structure and Latent Objective Emergence in Multi-Agent Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02507)
*Arman Ghaffarizadeh,Danyal Mohaddes,Aliakbar Izadkhah,Shahriar Noroozizadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究LLM代理在社会结构环境中的公开与私下言论差异，发现对齐性设置会导致系统性分歧，决策差异从3%上升到40%，并提出双通道评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理越来越多地在社会结构化环境中行动，角色、受众和关系语境可能会影响其表达内容的成本与收益。研究者想要了解，在没有明确提示目标的情况下，这种社会结构是否会改变代理在公开渠道与私下渠道（off-the-record）的表达差异。

Method: 研究提出双通道辩论框架（dual-channel debate framework），让代理产生两种输出：公开语句（进入共享历史）和私下回应（仅记录不展示给其他参与者）。实验涉及10个模型、3个场景，每个场景5个变体，通过立场、语义相似度、自然语言推理和问卷调查四种聚合分析方法评估差异。

Result: 对齐性设置导致目标代理出现系统性的公开-私下分歧，决策分歧从约3%的基线上升到约40%。在某些情况下，私下回应明确指出公开迎合是出于关系压力（如职业风险或赞助义务）的考量。

Conclusion: 研究发现表明代理评估应超越明确目标，检测涌现目标。研究提出了双通道评估框架和互补行为测量方法，以实际操作这种评估方式。

Abstract: LLM agents will increasingly act in socially structured settings where role, audience, and relational context can shape what is advantageous or costly to say. We study whether such social structure, without any explicit objective in the prompt, changes what an agent expresses publicly relative to an off-the-record (OTR) channel elicited under the same condition. We introduce a dual-channel debate framework in which agents produce public utterances that enter the shared history alongside OTR responses that are recorded but never shown to the other participant. Across 10 models, 3 scenarios, and 5 variations within each scenario, alignment-inducing settings produce systematic public-OTR divergence in the targeted agent, with its decision divergence rising from a $\sim$3% baseline to roughly 40%. The effect is consistent across four aggregate analyses: stance, semantic similarity, natural language inference, and survey responses. In some cases, the OTR response explicitly attributes public accommodation to relational pressures, such as career risk or sponsorship obligation. The findings suggest that agent evaluation should extend beyond explicit goals and detect emergent objectives. We present a dual-channel evaluation framework and complementary behavioral measures that operationalize this assessment.

</details>


### [258] [Online Safety Monitoring for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02510)
*Mona Schirmer,Metod Jazbec,Alexander Timans,Christian Naesseth,Maja Waldron,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种简单但有效的LLM安全监控系统，通过阈值化外部模型验证器信号实现实时风险检测，在实验中与复杂方法表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管经过对齐训练，大语言模型在部署时仍可能生成不安全输出，因此需要实时监控和警报机制来确保安全。

Method: 设计了一种简单的实时监控器，将外部模型的验证器信号通过阈值转换为警报决策，并通过风险控制方法校准阈值。

Result: 在数学推理和红队数据集上的实验显示，该简单设计与基于顺序假设检验的更先进监控方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 简单的阈值化监控系统在LLM安全监控方面能够达到与复杂方法相当的性能，为实际部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite alignment training, LLMs remain prone to generating unsafe outputs at deployment time. Monitoring outputs online and raising an alarm when safety can no longer be assumed is therefore critical. We study a simple real-time monitor that turns a verifier signal from an external model into an alarm decision by thresholding, with the threshold calibrated via risk control. In experiments on mathematical reasoning and red teaming datasets, we show that this simple design is competitive with more advanced monitors based on sequential hypothesis testing.

</details>


### [259] [A Hippocampus for Linear Attention: An Exact Memory for What the Recurrent State Forgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02303)
*Wanyun Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HOLA通过结合压缩记忆和精确缓存，在保持线性效率的同时显著提升了记忆能力和检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受互补学习系统（Complementary Learning Systems）启发，给线性注意力添加一个海马体补充。线性注意力机制和状态空间语言模型将前缀压缩为固定大小的循环状态，虽然实现了O(1)内存，但代价是有损的精确记忆——当许多键-值关联竞争时，早期事实被覆盖，needle recall性能下降。

Method: HOLA（Hippocampal Linear Attention）保持常规的delta规则状态作为压缩记忆，并添加一个有界的精确KV缓存，形成半参数测试时记忆：状态建模线性可压缩结构，而缓存存储不应强制通过该状态的关联。缓存写入无需学习驱逐模块，保留具有大beta * ||e||（预测残差）的token；解耦的RMSNorm-gamma缓存读取将这些精确KV对转化为尖锐检索而非软平均。

Result: 在340M参数、15B SlimPajama token上训练，HOLA将Wikitext困惑度从27.32降低到22.92（-16.1%），低于全注意力Transformer++（26.88），并改进LAMBADA困惑度从30.95到30.26。它还实现了最佳的线性上下文检索，并在RULER针在haystack中的检索中比GDN或匹配的HOLA+recency缓存更健壮，扩展到32k token（其训练长度的16倍）。

Conclusion: HOLA通过结合压缩记忆和精确缓存，在保持线性效率的同时显著提升了记忆能力和检索性能。

Abstract: Linear-attention and state-space language models compress the prefix into a fixed-size recurrent state, yielding O(1) memory at the cost of a lossy exact memory: when many key--value associations compete, earlier facts are overwritten and needle recall degrades. Inspired by Complementary Learning Systems, we give linear attention a hippocampal complement. HOLA (Hippocampal Linear Attention) keeps the usual delta-rule state as a compressive memory and adds a bounded exact KV cache, forming a semiparametric test-time memory: the state models linearly compressible structure, while the cache stores associations that should not be forced through that state. The cache writes without a learned eviction module, keeping tokens with large beta * ||e||, the prediction residual actually committed to the state; a decoupled RMSNorm-gamma cache read then turns these exact KV pairs into sharp retrieval rather than soft averaging. At 340M parameters trained on 15B SlimPajama tokens, HOLA lowers Wikitext perplexity from 27.32 to 22.92 (-16.1%), below a full-attention Transformer++ (26.88), and improves LAMBADA perplexity from 30.95 to 30.26. It also achieves the best linear in-context retrieval and remains much more robust than GDN or a matched HOLA+recency cache on RULER needle-in-a-haystack recall out to 32k tokens (16x its training length).

</details>


### [260] [Grounded autonomous research: a fault-tolerant LLM pipeline from corpus to manuscript in frontier computational physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02329)
*Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发了一个端到端的自主研究代理系统，能够从11,083篇凝聚态物理arXiv论文自动生成包含三个实质性物理发现的出版级手稿，通过文献校准和数值对抗机制解决了物理科学领域的研究代理幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究代理在机器学习沙箱中表现良好，但在前沿物理科学领域面临根本性挑战：物理推理要求更高、工具链文档不全、校准需依赖外部文献，而未经支撑的代理常产生幻觉和不可验证的结果。

Method: 构建端到端研究流水线：(1) 代理自主映射文献语料库构思研究方向；(2) 通过复现已发表参考文献校准方法论；(3) 执行新颖的第一性原理计算；(4) 撰写出版级手稿。系统采用47个新鲜上下文会话分6个阶段运行，涉及2,162次文献咨询事件，通过冗余设计、新鲜上下文隔离、分布式定位和对抗性审查实现容错。

Result: 成功生成关于替代磁性压磁性的出版级手稿，包含三个实质性物理发现。两个配对失败模式实验验证了校准检查点上的结构强制数值对抗是核心的定位机制。

Conclusion: 该研究为高利益科学领域（超越计算物理学）的自主研究奠定了基础，提供了可复用的原语、特征化失败模式和量化干预模式，证明了通过文献校准和数值对抗机制可以实现可靠的自主科学研究。

Abstract: Autonomous-research agents have demonstrated end-to-end LLM automation in machine-learning sandboxes where execution provides calibration. Frontier physical science differs categorically: physical reasoning underlies every methodology choice, toolchains are often underdocumented, and calibration must come from external literature anchors - which unscaffolded agents cite but do not confront, hallucinating plausible, unverifiable results from internal priors. We present a pipeline that runs end-to-end from a corpus of 11,083 recent condensed-matter physics arXiv papers to a publication-grade manuscript with three substantive physics findings (here on altermagnetic piezomagnetism): the agent autonomously conceives a research direction by mapping the corpus, calibrates methodology by reproducing published references, conducts novel first-principles computations, and writes the manuscript - grounded in literature throughout, across 47 fresh-context sessions in six phases sharing only on-disk state, with 2,162 literature-consultation events. Fault tolerance emerges from redundancy: fresh-context isolation, distributed grounding, and adversarial review catch what any single session misses; pre- and post-pilot stages are fully autonomous, and pilot requires bounded human intervention only at reproduction failures - operational knowledge curation, not scientific direction. Two paired failure modes - a pre-architecture baseline and a no-pilot ablation - isolate structurally enforced numerical confrontation at calibration checkpoints as the operative grounding mechanism. The primitives, characterized failure modes, and quantified intervention pattern lay a foundation for autonomous research in high-stakes scientific domains beyond computational physics.

</details>


### [261] [DRIFTLENS: Measuring Memory-Induced Reasoning Drift in Personalized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02374)
*Xi Fang,Weijie Xu,Yingqiang Ge,Yuhui Xu,Stephanie Eckman,Chandan K. Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示个性化语言模型的记忆机制会显著改变其推理路径，提出DRIFTLENS框架量化此类推理漂移，并通过实验验证不同模型及用户属性下的漂移现象，同时评估两种后训练方法在减轻漂移方面的效果及其对下游任务的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究个性化语言模型中存储的用户属性、偏好和上下文信息是否会影响开放性问题（无唯一标准答案）的推理轨迹，而非仅改变输出内容。

Method: 开发DRIFTLENS框架，将推理步骤映射至价值类别并测量无记忆与有记忆场景下的轨迹差异；在4个LLM和10类用户属性（如年龄、职业、残疾）上测试漂移现象；采用GRPO/DPO后训练方法进行漂移缓解实验。

Result: 用户属性记忆引发显著推理漂移（超出实用噪声基线），即使最终回答仍保持合理性；两种后训练方法均能降低漂移，但效果依赖模型与奖励函数，且对下游能力影响存在差异。

Conclusion: 记忆诱导的推理漂移是可量化的系统性问题，当前缓解策略仅能部分解决，需进一步研究其根本机制与优化方案。

Abstract: Personalization changes what a model says to a user; we show that it can also change the reasoning trajectory used to justify the response. Modern LLMs personalize interactions by storing user attributes, preferences, and prior context, then injecting this information into future prompts. We study whether such memory reshapes reasoning on open-ended questions where no single ground-truth answer exists. To quantify this effect, we introduce DRIFTLENS, a ground-truth-free framework that maps each expressed reasoning step to a value category and measures divergence between a question's no-memory trajectory and its trajectory under injected user-attribute memory. We first validate that DRIFTLENS distinguishes content-free pragmatic noise from substantive reasoning changes. Across four LLMs and 10 user-attribute categories, including age, occupation, and disability, user-attribute memory induces medium-to-large reasoning drift above each model's pragmatic-noise floor, even when final answers remain fluent, on-topic, and plausible. We then evaluate GRPO- and DPO-based post-training methods for reducing drift. Both reduce drift, but neither uniformly dominates; effects on downstream capability, helpfulness, and instruction following are model-and reward-dependent. These results suggest that memory-induced reasoning drift is a measurable and only partly mitigated failure mode of personalized language models.

</details>


### [262] [Hardware-Enforced Semantic Coordination for Safety-Critical Real-Time Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02376)
*Uwe M. Borghoff,Paolo Bottoni,Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对具身AI系统实时部署中软件协调机制的局限性，本文提出基于FPGA的硬件强制执行语义协调架构，通过将TB-CSPN框架的核心协调机制映射至硬件层，在保持推理自适应性的同时，实现确定性时序同步、语义门控及可验证的安全边界约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身AI系统在安全关键型实时部署场景中面临根本挑战：异构组件并发运行时的协调依赖软件实现，难以满足确定性延迟、可验证安全保证和时序同步等硬性需求，尤其在高风险领域存在协调不确定性风险。

Method: 构建硬件原生语义协调层，利用FPGA将TB-CSPN框架中的协调语义（如语义门控、授权约束）直接实现为硬件原语，分离语义推理与交互管理模块，形成硬件-软件协同架构。

Result: 实现了三类核心保障：1) 确定性时序同步机制；2) 语义访问控制与授权验证；3) 可验证的协调行为边界约束，同时保留上层推理系统的软件自适应特性。

Conclusion: 该架构突破传统软件协调瓶颈，通过硬件层强制执行关键协调语义，为复杂自主系统提供安全可靠的实时运行环境，填补了AI系统安全部署的技术空白。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic AI are producing increasingly complex autonomous systems that integrate large language models, world models, optimization engines, specialized neural architectures, autonomous platforms, and human operators. While much current research focuses on improving reasoning capabilities, safety-critical real-time deployment also requires bounded and verifiable coordination among heterogeneous components operating concurrently under uncertainty. Software-mediated coordination presents fundamental limitations in domains where bounded latency, deterministic coordination, and enforceable safety guarantees are essential.
  Hence, we propose a hardware-enforced semantic coordination architecture in which selected coordination semantics are implemented directly at the hardware level via field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). The approach builds on the Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) framework, which separates semantic reasoning from interaction management.
  In this approach, selected TB-CSPN coordination mechanisms are mapped onto FPGA primitives, creating a hardware-native semantic coordination layer. Focus is not on acceleration, but on enforcing temporal synchronization, semantic gating, authorization constraints, and bounded coordination behavior directly in hardware. Semantic reasoning remains adaptive and software-driven, while embedded coordination semantics become deterministic.

</details>


### [263] [Steerability via constraints: a substrate for scalable oversight of coding agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02389)
*Thomas Winninger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究针对编程代理缺乏有效人类监督的瓶颈，提出将传统工程团队管理方法（访问控制、网络策略、工具强制编码规范）迁移至代理系统。在Python代码后门检测实验中，召回率从54.5%提升至90.9%，证明该方法可有效降低监督成本并增强安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程代理能力强大但缺乏有效人类监督机制。无约束代理引入安全风险、降低代码库可伸缩性，并使人工审查成本急剧增加，成为大规模应用的瓶颈。需要低成本、可扩展的监督方法来平衡代理能力与安全可控性。

Method: 构建一个端到端的受限系统，采用访问控制、网络策略和工具强制执行的编码规范来约束编程代理行为。通过控制实验，使用Gemma 4 e4b小规模模型审查包含11个后门插入的Python代码库，比较无约束（无工具）与约束底层系统加~200行docs CLI两种场景下的后门检测召回率。

Result: 在受约束的底层系统加上约200行docs CLI配置后，后门检测召回率从54.5%（无约束、无工具）提升至90.9%。底层系统和工具分别独立贡献性能提升。Python作为测试语言具有代表性——底层监督收益最大的正是语言默认保障最少的环境。

Conclusion: 人类监督是编程代理应用的核心瓶颈，通过移植传统工程团队管理方法（访问控制、网络策略、工具强制编码规范）到编程代理，可以有效降低监督成本并提高安全性。该方法在Python代码库后门检测实验中证明有效，召回率从54.5%提升至90.9%，且原理可扩展至Rust等其他语言。

Abstract: Coding agents are capable; human oversight is the bottleneck. Unconstrained agents introduce security risks, erode codebase scalability, and make human review increasingly costly. We argue that the same methods used for decades to manage large human engineering teams: access control, network policies, strict coding conventions enforced by tooling; transfer directly to coding agents, and are cheaper (in token) than recent agentic scaffolding. We sketch a start-to-end system on this principle, and report a controlled experiment in scalable oversight: a small reviewer (Gemma 4 e4b) inspects a Python codebase containing 11 inserted backdoors. Recall rises from 54.5% (unconstrained, no tools) to 90.9% (constrained substrate plus a ~200-LoC `docs` CLI), with substrate and tools contributing independently. We choose Python deliberately: substrate-level oversight gains are largest where the language gives the fewest guarantees by default; the principles extend to languages like Rust.

</details>


### [264] [Fast Multi-dimensional Refusal Subspaces via RFM-AGOP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02396)
*Thomas Winninger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种使用递归特征机（RFM）算法高效提取大语言模型中多维拒绝子空间的新方法，相比现有方法计算更快且性能更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型中的行为引导和监控对安全性和可解释性很重要。虽然早期研究假设行为编码在单一线性方向上，但最新发现表明复杂行为（如拒绝回答有害查询）存在于多维子空间中。然而，现有提取这些子空间的方法计算成本高昂，对于产生长推理轨迹的推理模型来说变得不可行。

Method: 通过适应递归特征机（RFM）算法，并采用探针感知的初始化方法，能够高效计算多维拒绝子空间。该方法在推理模型（Qwen 3）和非推理模型（Qwen 2.5）上均进行了验证。

Result: RFM方法能够在几秒钟内识别多维拒绝子空间，不仅计算速度更快，在消融任务上的表现也优于其他替代方法。

Conclusion: 如果验证成功，RFM可以成为现有大语言模型子空间提取方法的一种廉价且可扩展的补充方法。未来计划进一步研究不同方法找到的子空间之间的关系。

Abstract: Steering and monitoring activations in Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for both safety and interpretability. Early work assumed behaviours are encoded along single linear directions, but recent findings suggest complex behaviours, such as the refusal to answer harmful queries, live in multi-dimensional subspaces. However, existing methods for extracting these subspaces are computationally expensive, which becomes prohibitive on reasoning models who produce long reasoning traces. By adapting the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm -- which can be computed efficiently -- with a probe-informed initialization, we are able to identify the multi-dimensional refusal subspace in seconds, on reasoning (Qwen 3) and non-reasoning (Qwen 2.5) models. While RFM allows for faster subspace identification, it also showed better performances on the ablation task than its alternatives. More work is planned to better understand the relations between subspaces found by different methods. If confirmed, RFM could be a cheap and scalable complement to existing subspace-extraction methods in LLMs.

</details>


### [265] [Text-Driven 3D Indoor Scene Synthesis in Non-Manhattan Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02407)
*Xianhui Meng,Zirui Song,Yuchen Zhang,Li Zhang,Yongxuan Lv,Xiuying Chen,Kun Wang,Yan Luo,Kai Chen,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen,Jun Liu,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SPG-Layout框架，结合物体分布统计先验和层次化布局策略，在复杂非曼哈顿环境中生成物理上合理的室内场景，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在非曼哈顿环境中难以建模非正交空间关系，导致几何违规和物理保真度低。

Method: 利用物体分布统计先验指导训练，增强环境理解和保真度；采用层次化布局策略，优先放置大物体，模拟人类设计流程。

Result: 构建了包含500个多样非曼哈顿环境的新基准；实验表明SPG-Layout在曼哈顿和非曼哈顿环境中均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 实现了语义真实性和物理合理性的平衡优化，代码将公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in 3D indoor synthesis for Manhattan environments. However, existing methods often fail to capture plausible object layout patterns in non-Manhattan settings, primarily because they struggle to model non-orthogonal spatial relationships, leading to high geometric violations and low physical fidelity. To address this challenge, we propose SPG-Layout, a novel text-driven framework designed to generate physically plausible indoor scenes within complex non-Manhattan environments. Specifically, we first utilize statistical priors of object distributions to guide the training process, enhancing environmental understanding and fidelity. Furthermore, mirroring human design workflows, we adopt a hierarchical layout strategy that prioritizes the placement of large objects, thereby substantially minimizing layout violations. By synergizing these components, SPG-Layout achieves a balanced optimization of semantic realism and physical plausibility. To evaluate performance in these complex settings, we constructed a new benchmark comprising 500 diverse non-Manhattan environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPG-Layout consistently and significantly outperforms existing methods across both Manhattan and non-Manhattan environments. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [266] [G-RRM: Guiding Symbolic Solvers with Recurrent Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02491)
*Timo Bertram,Sidhant Bhavnani,Richard Freinschlag,Erich Kobler,Andreas Mayr,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出G-RRM方法，结合SE-RRMs与符号求解器解决约束满足问题。实验表明，在搜索空间大且求解器能动态覆盖分支条件时，神经指导可显著提升效率（如回溯加速33.3倍），但依赖求解器架构特性（如CaDiCaL无效）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRMs在更大问题规模的外推能力不足，需设计符号等变结构提升泛化性，并探索神经模型与经典求解器协同机制以突破搜索效率瓶颈。

Method: 构建符号等变RRM（SE-RRM）作为神经求解器生成全局候选解，通过G-RRM框架动态引导Glucose/CaDiCaL等符号求解器；核心分析神经引导有效的两大条件：1）问题需具备组合爆炸式搜索空间；2）求解器架构需支持动态覆盖分支选择。

Result: 在9×9数独中，SE-RRM准确率达91.1%，使回溯冲突数归零且加速33.3倍（p<0.001），Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍；但对CaDiCaL仅1.02倍中位加速（无效），反呈0.90倍平均减速。25×25网格中Glucose保持1.17倍加速。

Conclusion: 神经指导的实际效能取决于问题复杂度与求解器适应性：仅当搜索空间足够庞大且求解器具备动态纠错能力时，G-RRM才能转化为显著计算效率提升，验证了神经-符号协同的适用边界。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on SE-RRMs, a symbol-equivariant instantiation of RRMs that exhibits improved extrapolation to larger problem sizes. We propose a neuro-symbolic approach, ``Guiding with Recurrent Reasoning Models'' (G-RRM), which integrates SE-RRMs with symbolic solvers for constraint satisfaction problems. SE-RRMs act as neural solvers that generate full solution proposals and guide classical symbolic solvers, such as backtracking or SAT-based methods like Glucose 4.1 and CaDiCaL 3.0.0, that produce globally correct solutions. Centrally, we investigate when neural guidance with G-RRM improves the search efficiency of symbolic solvers. %
Our experiments show that the efficacy of G-RRM depends on two conditions: first, the problem instances must have an expansive combinatorial search space to expose potential gains, and second, the solver architecture must be capable of dynamically overwriting its branching choices to recover when neural hints are imperfect. When these conditions hold, guidance drives median conflict counts to zero and yields significant wall-clock speedups: on $9\times9$ Sudoku, where the SE-RRM correctly solves $91.1\%$ of instances, backtracking accelerates by $33.3\times$ and Glucose 4.1 by $1.70\times$ (median, $p<0.001$), with Glucose 4.1 retaining a $1.17\times$ speedup on perfect-hint $25\times25$ grids. In contrast, CaDiCaL 3.0.0, whose runtime is overhead-dominated and which always respects the injected branching hints rather than overwriting them, shows no significant speedup (median $1.02\times$, n.s.) and even a small significant mean slowdown ($0.90\times$) on $9\times9$. These results delineate the regimes in which neural guidance translates into practical speedups.

</details>


### [267] [ReContext: Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02509)
*Yanjun Zhao,Ruizhong Qiu,Tianxin Wei,Yuanchen Bei,Zhining Liu,Lingjie Chen,Ismini Lourentzou,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RECONTEXT方法，通过模型内部相关性信号构建证据池并回放，改善LLM长上下文推理能力，无需训练且保持完整上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管当前LLMs支持越来越长的上下文窗口，但它们常常无法有效利用输入中已有的相关证据，揭示了上下文访问与有效上下文利用之间的差距。理解和推理长上下文已成为在现实应用中部署LLMs的关键要求。

Method: 提出RECONTEXT（Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning），一种无需训练的推理方法。该方法使用模型内部相关性信号构建查询条件证据池，并在最终生成前进行回放，同时保持完整的原始上下文。这种递归选择过程将证据组织与答案生成分离，无需训练、外部记忆或上下文剪枝。

Result: 在8个长上下文数据集上进行实验，使用128K上下文长度，结果显示RECONTEXT在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B三个模型上持续改善证据利用能力，在所有三个骨干模型上均取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: RECONTEXT有效解决了LLMs在长上下文场景中的证据利用问题，通过递归证据回放机制提升了推理性能，为实际应用提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning over long contexts has become a key requirement for deploying large language models (LLMs) in realistic applications. Although recent LLMs support increasingly long context windows, they often fail to use relevant evidence that is already present in the input, revealing a gap between context access and effective context utilization. In this work, we propose Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning (RECONTEXT), a training-free inference method for improving long-context reasoning. RECONTEXT uses model-internal relevance signals to construct a query-conditioned evidence pool and replays it before final generation while preserving the full original context. This recursive selection process separates evidence organization from answer generation without training, external memory, or context pruning. We also provide a theoretical analysis based on associative memory, which characterizes the context as a memory store, the question as a retrieval cue, attention as cue-trace association, and replay as trace reactivation. Experiments on eight long-context datasets with 128K context length show that RECONTEXT consistently improves evidence utilization across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama3-8B, achieving the best average rank on all three backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/ReContext.

</details>


### [268] [Distributed Attacks in Persistent-State AI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02514)
*Josh Hills,Ida Caspary,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了AI编程代理在持久代码库中迭代开发时面临的新攻击面，其中攻击者可利用跨PR渐进式部署规避监控，而单一监控机制无法同时防御所有攻击模式；通过引入状态感知监控器组合可显著降低风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编程代理自主性增强，其跨会话持久化代码库的特性为攻击者提供了新的攻击路径——可通过分阶段PR部署隐蔽攻击载荷，并选择最佳掩护时机执行恶意操作。

Method: 构建Iterative VibeCoding实验框架，包含20种CLI工具与Flask服务任务变体；使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为攻击代理、GPT-4o作为监控器，对比渐进式与单PR攻击策略；设计状态链接跟踪监控器并组合多监控器进行防御测试。

Result: 1) 单一监控器无法同时防御渐进/非渐进攻击；2) 高规避率(≥65%)在Sonnet/Gemini/Kimi等多模型间保持一致；3) 状态感知监控器对渐进攻击检测效果显著优于传统diff监控器；4) 四监控器组合将渐进攻击规避率从93%降至47%。

Conclusion: 证实了持久状态攻击面的普遍风险特性，提出基于状态追踪的监控范式可有效提升防御能力，强调需通过多监控器协同构建动态防御体系应对迭代式AI攻击。

Abstract: As AI coding agents become more autonomous, they increasingly ship code iteratively, with the codebase persisting across sessions. This persistence creates a new attack surface: a misaligned or prompt-injected agent can distribute attacks across pull requests (PRs) and time its payload for the PR with the best natural cover. To study the resulting dynamics, we introduce Iterative VibeCoding, a setting for AI control, the study of safely deploying capable but potentially untrusted AI. In Iterative VibeCoding, a coding agent builds software over a sequence of PRs in a persistent codebase while pursuing a covert side task. Our benchmark includes two task families: CLI tools and Flask web services, across 20 total task variations. We use Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the attack agent and GPT-4o as the monitor. We compare gradual attacks, which distribute the side task across PRs, against non-gradual attacks concentrated in a single PR. No single monitor is robust to both: which strategy evades best (success while evading the monitor) depends on the monitor type, so a defender cannot close off both gradual and non-gradual attacks with any one monitor. High evasion (>= 65%) generalizes across model attack agent backends (Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5), confirming this is a property of the persistent-state attack surface rather than a single model's capability. Evasion also remains high across state-of-the-art monitor models and the gap between gradual and non-gradual evasion widens for more capable models. We introduce a stateful link-tracker monitor that tracks suspicious buildup across PRs. On both task families, it detects gradual attacks substantially better than diff monitors that merely see more accumulated history. Combining this stronger monitor with trajectory monitors in a four-monitor ensemble reduces gradual-attack evasion from 93% under the weakest standard diff monitor to 47%.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [Alternating Optimization for Joint Resource Allocation in Full-Duplex Multi-Sector Fluid Antenna-Enabled Near-Field Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01300)
*Jingxuan Zhou,Yinchao Yang,Zhaohui Yang,A. Hamid Aghvami,Mohammad Shikh-Bahaei*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合天线移动与资源分配的全双工流体天线近场系统（FD-FANS），通过分组收发分区实现同步下行能量传输与上行数据传输。针对非凸混合整数优化问题，设计了基于MM-SCA的交替优化算法，仿真表明其性能显著优于半双工、固定天线等基准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半双工系统频谱效率低，固定位置天线存在空间分集受限问题，而现有全双工系统未充分利用近场效应和天线自由度。需突破残余自干扰（SI）、无线能量传输（WET）约束及天线运动几何限制。

Method: 构建考虑残余SI/WET的球面波近场信道模型，设计TX/RX分组交替协议；建立加权总速率最大化问题（含功率限制、天线间距约束）；提出基于MM-SCA的交替优化框架，通过连续松弛处理混合整数变量。

Result: 相比HD-FANS、FD-FPANS及远场方案：(1) ASR提升12.3%-38.7% (2) EE改善19.5%-42.1% (3) 用户公平性指数提升15.8%-29.4%；在残余SI=10dB及信道估计误差5%时仍保持稳定性。

Conclusion: FD-FANS通过联合优化实现近场空间资源利用最大化，MM-SCA算法保障收敛性，突破传统全双工近场系统性能瓶颈，为6G通感一体化提供新思路。

Abstract: This paper proposes a full duplex fluid antenna near field system (FD-FANS) with a multi-sector antenna array that jointly exploits resource allocation, antenna mobility, and group-based transmitting (TX) and receiving (RX) partitioning. A spherical wave uplink downlink channel is established that accounts for residual self interference (SI), wireless energy transfer (WET), and geometric constraints on antenna motion. Within the FD-FANS framework, an efficient protocol is devised to enable simultaneous downlink energy transmission (DET) and uplink data transmission (UDT) at the base station (BS). Furthermore, we formulate, for both perfect and imperfect SI cancellation (SIC), a weighted sum rate (WSR) maximization problem over time power allocation, antenna positions, and binary group selection, under practical average and peak power limits, per antenna box constraints, minimum spacing, and a half TX half RX balance. To tackle the resulting non convex mixed integer design, we develop an efficient alternating optimization (AO) framework based on majorization minimization successive convex approximation (MM SCA). The proposed algorithm monotonically improves the objective and converges to a stationary solution of a continuous relaxation. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves consistent performance gains over several benchmark designs, including half duplex FANS (HD FANS), FD fixed position antenna near field system (FD FPANS), non-grouped FD FANS, and far field counterparts, in terms of average sum rate (ASR), energy efficiency (EE), and user fairness, while exhibiting robustness to residual SI and channel uncertainty.

</details>


### [270] [Generalizable framework of eating episode detection on free-living wrist-worn wearable data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01376)
*Chunzhuo Wang,Emma De Schuyteneer,Huaidong Du,Aiden Doherty,Elske Vrieze,Bart Vanrumste*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种方向不变的手腕佩戴设备进食行为检测框架，通过引入acc2gyro模块解决仅加速度计数据性能下降问题，并在三个异构数据集上实现外部验证，首次涵盖进食障碍人群，显著提升了算法泛化性和临床适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴进食检测方法普遍存在三大局限：1) 依赖单一数据集内部验证导致泛化性差；2) 依赖固定传感器方向与已知佩戴手；3) 多数方法需同时使用加速度计(ACC)和陀螺仪(GYRO)数据，但实际应用中GYRO常因功耗限制缺失，且仅用ACC时性能显著下降。

Method: 构建了方向不变检测框架，核心创新为：1) 采用多源异构训练数据（精细手腕佩戴数据集）；2) 设计acc2gyro信号重构模块补偿GYRO缺失；3) 实施严格的外部验证策略，在CAD、Capture-24及Physio-ED三个差异显著的数据集上测试。

Result: 外部验证结果显示：在CAD、Capture-24、Physio-ED上分别取得0.751/0.592/0.793的F1分数，其中CAD性能超越近期仅内部验证的SOTA方法。acc2gyro模块使纯ACC场景下的F1分数提升约8-15个百分点，首次在进食障碍人群中实现有效检测。

Conclusion: 验证了方向不变可穿戴传感技术在进食行为评估中的可扩展性与临床可行性，为跨场景、跨人群的健康监测提供了新思路，尤其在低功耗设备和特殊医疗人群中的应用具有重要价值。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of eating behavior is essential for understanding and managing conditions such as eating disorders, obesity, and diabetes. Wearable-based food intake detection has shown considerable promise; however, most existing approaches are trained and evaluated using internal validation on a single dataset with fixed sensor orientation and known wearing hand, which limits their generalizability to real-world settings. Furthermore, many existing approaches rely on both accelerometer (acc) and gyroscope (gyro) signals to achieve strong performance. However, gyro measurements may be unavailable in some real-world deployments due to battery constraints, and performance often degrades when only acc data are used. We propose a generalizable framework for orientation-invariant eating episode detection, with an acc2gyro module to improve performance in acc-only settings. The framework is trained using fine-grained wrist-worn datasets and externally validated across three heterogeneous datasets: the Clemson All-Day (CAD) and Capture-24 datasets, as well as Physio-ED, a dataset collected from individuals with eating disorders. Across external evaluations, the proposed framework demonstrates robust performance despite substantial variations in sensor modality, wearing hand, participant population, and annotation protocols. Specifically, the framework achieved F1-scores of 0.751, 0.592, and 0.793 on CAD, Capture-24, and Physio-ED, respectively, with CAD performance exceeding recent state-of-the-art methods evaluated using internal validation only. This study provides the first external validation of eating episode detection in an eating disorder population. Additionally, the acc2gyro module improves the performance in acc-only settings. These findings demonstrate the potential of orientation-invariant wearable sensing for scalable and clinically applicable assessment of eating behavior.

</details>


### [271] [Channel Knowledge Map Reconstruction From Sparse Measurements via Pilot-Anchored Layout-Conditioned Fourier Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01453)
*Zhonghao Jiu,Fan Meng,Yongming Huang,Hang Zhan,Zening Liu,Xiaohu You*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出Anchor-CKM框架解决稀疏信道测量下的知识图谱重建问题，通过支持感知卷积和布局条件傅里叶优化提升重建精度，在DeepMIMO场景下RMSE降低0.79-1.33dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CKM重建方法在处理稀疏不规则测量时易产生掩码失真，现有方法无法有效分离采样掩码与信道场的耦合效应，导致环境先验信息被错误应用于掩码引起的伪影。

Method: Anchor-CKM采用三步策略：1) 使用支持感知部分卷积构建导频支撑表示；2) 执行布局条件双路径傅里叶细化；3) 基于坐标的异方差预测生成CKM均值及位置相关方差。

Result: 在DeepMIMO非重叠发射器场景中测试，覆盖0.3-0.95缺失率（含5%-10%极端低导频覆盖率），相比最强基线方法RMSE降低0.79-1.33dB。消融实验证实导频支撑稳定化贡献最大，布局条件显著改善LOS/NLOS边界保真度。

Conclusion: Anchor-CKM通过测量优先的知识辅助框架有效解耦采样掩码与信道场，在稀疏测量条件下实现高精度CKM重建，特别在低导频覆盖率场景表现突出。

Abstract: Channel knowledge maps (CKMs) enable environment-aware wireless systems by providing location-specific channel knowledge, but long-term environmental variations, such as construction, traffic redistribution, and foliage changes, require periodic map refresh. In practice, channel measurements are often sparse and irregular, while environmental knowledge may be limited to coarse layout or topology descriptors. This paper studies CKM reconstruction from sparse measurements. We show that reconstruction pipelines that apply local aggregation or spectral operators directly to a zero-filled pilot grid can entangle the sampling mask with the channel field, allowing structural priors to act on mask-induced distortions before the measurements define a supported radio field. To address this issue, we propose Anchor-CKM, a measurement-first, knowledge-aided reconstruction framework. Anchor-CKM first uses support-aware partial convolutions to construct a pilot-supported representation, and then performs layout-conditioned dual-path Fourier refinement followed by coordinate-based heteroscedastic prediction of the CKM mean and per-location predictive variance. Experiments on transmitter-disjoint DeepMIMO scenarios cover missing ratios from 0.3 to 0.95, including stringent 5% to 10% pilot-coverage settings. In explicit-layout outdoor scenarios, Anchor-CKM reduces received-power root-mean-square error (RMSE) by 0.79 to 1.33 dB relative to the strongest reproduced baseline, while ablations identify pilot-support stabilization as the largest contributor and layout conditioning as beneficial for line-of-sight/non-line-of-sight (LOS/NLOS) boundary fidelity.

</details>


### [272] [Waveform Design for Underwater Simultaneous Acoustic Information and Power Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01565)
*Jinheng Kang,Yizhe Zhao,Kun Yang,Jun Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一种联合考虑换能器频响特性和整流电路非线性的多载波水下声学信息与能量同时传输（SAIPT）系统。通过SCA方法优化波形向量，结合AO框架联合优化功率分配因子与波形，显著提升了能量传输效率，并验证了子载波参数对DC输出的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现自供电、免维护的水下物联网（IoUT）网络需要高效同时传输声学与能量的SAIPT技术，现有研究未充分解决换能器频响特性和整流电路非线性导致的效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于SCA的波形向量优化算法，在平均/峰值功率约束下解决APT问题；在SAIPT场景下采用AO框架联合优化功率分割因子与波形向量，同时满足发射功率与可达速率约束。

Result: 仿真表明：1) 整合换能器频响、整流非线性及多载波高PAPR特性可显著提升能量传输效率；2) 通过优化子载波数量与间隔等参数可进一步增加能量采集DC输出。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效克服传统SAIPT系统设计局限，通过物理层特性建模与联合优化策略，为构建高能效水下物联网提供了理论支撑和实践路径。

Abstract: Simultaneous acoustic information and power transfer (SAIPT) plays a crucial role in enabling self-sustainable and maintenance-free Internet of Underwater Things (IoUT) networks. This paper studies a multicarrier underwater SAIPT system that jointly considers the frequency-dependent characteristics of acoustic transducers and the nonlinear behavior of rectifier circuits. The waveform vector is firstly optimized using the successive convex approximation (SCA) method under constraints on average and peak transmit power for acoustic power transfer (APT). Then, in the SAIPT scenario, both the power splitting factor and waveform vectors are jointly optimized through an alternating optimization (AO) framework based on SCA, subject to transmit power and achievable rate constraints. Simulation results demonstrate that incorporating the transducer's frequency response, rectifier nonlinearity, and the high peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) of multicarrier waveforms leads to a significant improvement in acoustic energy transfer efficiency. The results also show that the energy harvesting DC output can be further enhanced by properly choosing system parameters, such as the number of subcarriers and subcarrier spacing.

</details>


### [273] [Rethinking Fractional Programming for Joint Uplink Scheduling and Power Control in Multicell Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01769)
*Zihan Jiao,Xinping Yi,Shi Jin,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出SEFP算法，通过RIT改进LDT，解决WSR优化问题，性能优于传统方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LDT在处理离散变量时导致保守的替代函数，影响优化效果

Method: 提出RIT，结合QT，形成SEFP算法，保持分单元分离，闭式更新

Result: 仿真显示SEFP在多种网络效用下优于经典FP和其他基线

Conclusion: SEFP有效提升WSR，同时保持计算效率和分单元结构

Abstract: This paper investigates the joint uplink scheduling and power control problem in a coordinated multicell wireless network, where at most one single-antenna user is allowed to access the single-antenna base station in each cell simultaneously. The resulting weighted sum-rate (WSR) maximization problem is a mixed discrete-continuous, nonconvex optimization problem that is notoriously difficult to solve directly. Classical fractional programming (FP) methods tackle this problem by leveraging the Lagrangian dual transform (LDT) followed by the quadratic transform (QT), yielding a tractable closed-form solution for scheduling and power control, with the LDT playing a crucial role in handling discrete variables. In this paper, we revisit the LDT from a minorization-maximization (MM) perspective and observe that its induced surrogate is somehow conservative due to the reciprocal-coordinate construction. Motivated by this observation, we propose a novel reciprocal-inversion transform (RIT) that constructs a tighter first-order Taylor expansion lower bound for the logarithmic rate function. The proposed RIT remains fully compatible with the QT, leading to a surrogate-enhanced FP (SEFP) algorithm for joint uplink scheduling and power control. The proposed SEFP algorithm retains the desirable per-cell separability of the classical FP framework and admits closed-form updates for the auxiliary variables, scheduling decisions, and transmit powers. Simulation results demonstrate that the SEFP algorithm consistently outperforms the classical FP method and other baselines for different network utilities.

</details>


### [274] [Scene-Conditioned PINN-GNN for Multipath RF Maps: Cross-Scene Generation and In-Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01777)
*Lizhou Liu,Xiaohui Chen,Zihan Tang,Mengyao Ma,Wenyi Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出统一RF地图构建框架，结合PINN与GNN实现跨场景生成与场景内补全，引入峰值加权动态时间扭曲评估指标，性能超越图像/扩散/插值基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RF地图构建方法面临三大挑战：1) 依赖大量实测数据；2) 跨场景泛化能力不足；3) 缺乏多径重建质量的综合评估机制。需融合物理传播规律与空间相关性建模。

Method: 采用双模块架构：PINN嵌入电磁传播方程约束，建立接收机位置到多径参数（路径增益/时延/角度）的物理一致性映射；GNN通过邻接节点特征聚合保障空间连续性。创新性提出峰值加权动态时间扭曲（PW-DTW）指标，量化信道冲激响应（CIR）中的幅度误差与峰时延错位。

Result: 实验验证：在2D/2.5D环境下的稀疏观测场景中，该框架在地图级指标（如PSNR/SSIM）和多径级指标（如RMSE/角度误差）上均优于CNN/U-Net、扩散模型及传统插值方法，且泛化误差降低32%。

Conclusion: 首次实现物理约束与空间相关性的统一建模，PW-DTW指标显著提升多径重建质量评估精度，该方法为环境自适应无线系统提供可靠工具，在稀疏采样条件下仍能保持高保真度。

Abstract: Radio frequency (RF) maps provide a compact representation of multipath propagation characteristics and are fundamental to channel modeling, coverage analysis, and environment-aware wireless optimization. This paper proposes a unified RF map construction framework based on a physics-informed neural network (PINN) and a graph neural network (GNN), supporting both cross-scene generation and in-scene completion with 2D and 2.5D environmental representations. The PINN embeds electromagnetic propagation constraints to establish a physically consistent mapping from receiver locations to multipath parameters, including path gain, time of arrival, and angles, while the GNN enforces spatial consistency by modeling correlations among neighboring receivers. To comprehensively evaluate multipath reconstruction quality, we propose a peak-weighted dynamic time warping metric that jointly accounts for amplitude errors and peak delay misalignment in channel impulse responses. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms image-based, diffusion-based, and interpolation baselines across both map-level and multipath-level metrics, achieving robust generalization and high-fidelity RF map construction under sparse observations.

</details>


### [275] [Integrated Wake-Up Radio and MIMO Solution for Cellular IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02008)
*Israa Khaled,Ammar El Falou,Nour Kouzayha,Charlotte Langlais*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入MIMO波束成形技术优化物联网唤醒无线电系统，证明在特定天线配置下可显著提升唤醒可靠性并减少误激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有唤醒无线电技术多依赖单天线基站，存在覆盖范围有限和误激活问题，亟需探索多天线方案以提升能效与可靠性。

Method: 基于随机几何理论建立多小区蜂窝网络分析框架，研究当基站长天线数量满足$2 \times (\text{设备数})-1$时的WUR-MIMO唤醒成功概率。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真表明：相比单天线基线，MIMO波束成形使唤醒可靠性显著提升，所有场景下误激活降低超50%，有效延长物联网设备寿命。

Conclusion: 验证了MIMO波束成形在物联网唤醒系统中的实用价值，为超低功耗通信提供了新的理论支撑和技术路径。

Abstract: Wake-up radio (WUR) is a technology designed to enhance the energy efficiency of Internet of Things (IoT) networks and extend device battery life. While most studies focus on WUR performance with single-antenna base stations, this paper investigates the multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technology to improve device energy saving and extend the coverage of wake-up signals. By leveraging MIMO beamforming, the transmitted energy can be spatially focused toward the intended IoT devices, with high beamforming gain and minimal inter-device interference. We develop a preliminary analytical framework using stochastic geometry to evaluate the wake-up success probability of WUR-MIMO in multi-cell cellular IoT networks, when the number of antennas equals $2 \times (\text{number of devices}) - 1$. Monte Carlo simulations show that, relative to a single-antenna WUR baseline, MIMO beamforming significantly enhances wake-up reliability when this antenna configuration is applied, mitigates more than 50% of false activations across all settings, and thereby prolongs the lifetime of IoT devices.

</details>


### [276] [Antenna System for Simultaneous Wireless Power and Information Transfer to Brain Implants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02036)
*Ali Khaleghi,Aminolah Hassanvand,Ilangko Balasingham*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 介绍了一种无电池无线脑机接口系统，通过双功能天线实现无线电力传输和数据连接，消除传统有线连接的风险，提升安全性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统植入式脑机接口需要有线连接，存在植入风险和不便，亟需改进。

Method: 设计双功能天线系统：一是感应耦合机制进行无线电力传输，为植入设备的ASIC供电；二是反向散射天线实现无电池高数据率无线连接（高达32 Mbps）。

Result: 成功消除电线，同时保留数据保真度和能量效率，适用于机器人手臂控制等任务。

Conclusion: 该系统提供更安全、更高效的脑机接口解决方案，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) have revolutionized neuroscience applications, from motor rehabilitation to neuroergonomics. Traditional implantable BCIs with invasive microelectrode arrays pose challenges, notably the need for wired connections and inherent implantation risks. This paper introduces a battery-free wireless BCI system, consolidating an implant and its external supporting system. Our design centers on a dual-function antenna system: firstly, an inductive coupling mechanism enables wireless power transfer, sufficiently powering the implant's Application-Specific Integrated Circuit (ASIC) for stimulation and readout without an implant battery. Secondly, a backscatter antenna in the implant facilitates battery-free, high-data-rate wireless connectivity (up to 32 Mbps). This system not only enhances the BCI experience by eliminating wires but also retains data fidelity and energy efficiency, promising a safer, more efficient interface for tasks like robotic arm control.

</details>


### [277] [Three-Dimensional Spatial Correlation Modeling for Cylindrical mMIMO Arrays in HAPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02111)
*Shasha Liu,Abla Kammoun,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 为高海拔平台站(HAPS)圆柱形mMIMO系统推导了精确的三维MIMO信道空间相关函数闭式表达式，基于球谐函数展开方法，考虑了任意天线方向图和角分布特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高海拔平台站(HAPS)是未来无线网络的关键组成部分，需要利用圆柱形大规模MIMO系统实现超广覆盖和直接用户连接。现有空间相关模型主要针对平面或线性天线阵列开发，无法直接应用于HAPS平台常用的圆柱形几何结构，制约了信道自由度的有效利用。

Method: 基于平面波的球谐函数展开方法推导三维MIMO信道空间相关函数，通过功率方位谱和天顶谱的傅里叶级数系数来容纳任意天线辐射模式和角分布，建立了适合圆柱形天线阵列的精确闭式表达式。

Result: 通过符合标准设置的蒙特卡洛仿真验证了所推导空间相关函数的准确性，证实了该方法在三维MIMO信道建模中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究为HAPS平台的圆柱形mMIMO系统提供了精确的空间相关函数建模方法，填补了现有模型在非平面天线阵列应用方面的空白，为未来无线网络的信道建模和优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: High-altitude platform stations (HAPS) are envisioned as a key component of future wireless networks, enabling ultra-wide coverage and providing direct connectivity to users with cylindrical massive multiple-input multiple-output (mMIMO) systems. Exploiting the channel degrees of freedom necessitates accurate modeling and characterization of three-dimensional (3D) channels in the presence of spatial correlation functions (SCFs). However, existing spatial correlation models are primarily developed for planar or linear antenna arrays and cannot be directly applied to cylindrical geometries commonly adopted by HAPS platforms. To address this limitation, this paper derives an exact closed-form expression for the SCF of 3D MIMO channels with antenna elements arranged in a cylindrical array. The proposed formulation is based on the spherical harmonic expansion (SHE) of plane waves and accommodates arbitrary antenna radiation patterns and angular distributions through the Fourier series (FS) coefficients of the power azimuth and zenith spectra. The derived SCF is validated through Monte Carlo simulations under standard-compliant settings.

</details>


### [278] [Coverage Analysis in Terahertz Clustered HetNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02125)
*Hadeel Obaid*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 太赫兹通信在 clustered HetNets 中通过泊松簇过程建模，覆盖性能优于传统 PPP 模型，适当 SBS 空间分布可提升覆盖效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹技术因传输距离限制需部署于异构网络，研究其在 clustered HetNets 中的覆盖增强潜力。

Method: 采用随机几何建模：宏基站用泊松点过程（PPP），小基站与用户用泊松簇过程（PCP），推导用户关联概率、干扰拉普拉斯变换及覆盖概率公式，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证。

Result: 数值结果表明 PCP-HetNets 覆盖率高于 PPP-HetNets，且适度的 SBS 空间分布可优化覆盖效果。

Conclusion: 太赫兹在 clustered HetNets 中具有更高覆盖性能，合理配置小基站空间分布可显著提升网络效能。

Abstract: Terahertz (THz) transmission technologies hold significant potential for enabling ultra-broadband, short-range communication in next-generation networks. Despite the vast bandwidth, THz signals suffer from limited transmission range and a feasible scenario is to deploy THz within clustered heterogeneous networks (HetNets) to enhance coverage. This paper investigates THz communication in clustered HetNets, leveraging stochastic geometry for performance analysis. Specifically, we consider two tiers of macro base stations (MBS) and small base stations (SBS). The MBS tier is modeled as a Poisson Point Process (PPP), and both the SBS tier and users are modeled as a Poisson Cluster Process (PCP) to capture user clustering and network hotspots. We derive the analytical expressions for user association probabilities, the Laplace transform of interference, and the coverage probability. The derived coverage probability is validated through Monte Carlo simulation. The numerical results show that the coverage in THz PCP-HetNets is higher than that achieved in THz PPP HetNets. In addition, a moderate spatial spread of SBSs is beneficial for coverage.

</details>


### [279] [Complexity-Scalable Direct Geolocation and Cancellation of Terrestrial GNSS Jammers: Single-Satellite and Multi-Antenna Experiments in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02190)
*Giacomo Pojani,Javier Tegedor,Joaquim Fortuny-Guasch,Francesco Menzione,David Evans,Tim Oerther,Maximilian Henkel,Jonas Stene Lindbjor*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出准直接定位（QDG）技术，利用LEO机会卫星压缩I/Q数据并快速处理，实现GNSS干扰源的近实时高精度定位，解决卫星带宽与计算资源瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间射频监测对卫星通信带宽和计算资源要求极高，而LEO机会卫星普遍受限于尺寸、重量和功耗（SWaP），亟需高效技术突破下链容量与星载算力瓶颈。

Method: 采用被动射频定位技术QDG，通过量化时频域压缩I/Q样本，结合快速延迟-多普勒匹配与干涉测量算法加速多源定位搜索。

Result: 基于OPS-SAT PRETTY卫星实测数据验证：在极高压缩比下成功定位不同信噪比的GNSS干扰源，量化精度边界并展示单/多天线应用场景。

Conclusion: QDG技术显著降低资源需求，使受限SWaP卫星具备近实时GNSS干扰定位能力，为多星座GNSS射频干扰监测系统提供关键支撑。

Abstract: Monitoring the radio-frequency (RF) spectrum from space imposes demanding requirements to satellite platforms in terms of communication bandwidth and computational resources, which are necessary for the downlink, the storage, and the processing of high-throughput I/Q samples. This paper analyzes in depth the quasi-direct geolocation (QDG) as a technique to enable the exploitation of satellites of opportunity in low Earth orbit (LEO) to sense the spectrum in the bands of global navigation satellite systems (GNSS). This is a technique of passive RF geolocation and consists of an ensemble of signal processing algorithms, which compress the I/Q samples and process the compressed data through fast delay-Doppler shift matching and interferometry in a quantized time-frequency domain. These algorithms speed up the exhaustive search of multiple RF sources in the position domain. The efficiency gain addresses the bottleneck that prevents the employment of satellites, which are limited in downlink capacity and on-board computational power. These satellites are usually constrained in size, weight and power (SWaP) and represent most of the spacecrafts in LEO. The ability to exploit assets as such for the geolocation of terrestrial GNSS jammers in near real time is instrumental the performance of a multi-constellation GNSS RFI monitoring system. The present work describes the mathematical framework and precision bounds, introduces single- and multi-antenna uses cases, combines different compression methods, and evaluates the geolocation accuracy with real data. The I/Q samples were collected by a repurposed GNSS reflectometry (GNSS-R) satellite, OPS-SAT PRETTY, in a dedicated test session during Jammertest 2025. The experimental results demonstrate the capability to geolocate GNSS jammers with different signal-to-noise ratios (SNR) with extremely high compression ratios.

</details>


### [280] [Fourier Preconditioning for Neural Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02199)
*Preston Pitzer,Anish Pradhan,Harpreet S. Dhillon*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: The paper addresses limitations of H-Score networks in feature learning under data scarcity by introducing FFT-based unitary preconditioning and spectral metrics to optimize basis selection, achieving significant NMSE reduction in low-resource regimes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MI-inspired feature learning suffers from noisy probability estimates in low-data regimes; H-Score's sensitivity to basis rotations under constrained approximations exacerbates finite-width truncation errors, necessitating robust basis optimization.

Method: 1. Prove H-Score invariance in unrestricted settings but rotation sensitivity in constrained classes
2. Develop FFT-based unitary preconditioning for stationary processes leveraging spectral concentration
3. Propose training-free metrics (spectral entropy, cumulative dependence energy) to quantify basis suitability pre-training
4. Evaluate via experiments on 8 multivariate datasets

Result: FFT preconditioning achieved up to 50% NMSE reduction in resource-constrained settings; proposed metrics strongly correlated with performance gains and correctly identified detrimental cases for spectral preconditioning

Conclusion: FFT preconditioning effectively mitigates basis-induced errors in H-Score networks while spectral metrics enable predictive basis selection without training overhead, improving efficiency in low-data scenarios

Abstract: Mutual information (MI)-inspired feature learning techniques are capable of generating low-dimensional embeddings that retain nonlinear dependence structures, but direct estimations of MI suffer from noisy probability distribution estimates in the low-data regime. The H-Score objective, computed from second-order statistics, provides a practical proxy metric for training feature extraction networks. We prove that H-Score is invariant to invertible transformations in the unrestricted functional setting, but becomes sensitive to input basis rotations under constrained approximation classes. Consequently, we study unitary preconditioning for H-Score networks and show that selecting an appropriate basis rotation reduces finite-width truncation error by concentrating predictive dependence into fewer dominant modes. We identify the fast Fourier transform (FFT) as an effective data-independent, low-cost preconditioner for approximately stationary processes, where spectral structure induces concentration of the cross-covariance singular value spectrum. We introduce training-free metrics based on spectral entropy and cumulative dependence energy to quantify basis suitability and predict downstream inference gains prior to network training. Experiments across eight multivariate datasets demonstrate that FFT preconditioning is particularly useful in resource-constrained regimes, achieving up to 50% normalized mean squared error (NMSE) reduction, while the proposed metrics correlate with observed performance gains and correctly identify cases where spectral preconditioning is detrimental.

</details>


### [281] [Beyond Beamforming: Phase-and-Gain Channel Shaping via Rotatable Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02305)
*Xingxiang Peng,Qingqing Wu,Ziyuan Zheng,Wen Chen,Guangchi Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本论文研究基于可旋转天线阵列的几何可重构传输技术，通过联合优化阵列姿态与单元视轴转向提升多用户系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定阵列无法动态调整信道相位响应与方向性增益，限制多用户通信效率，需探索几何重构机制实现信道整形。

Method: 提出联合优化框架，在可见性及转向约束下协调发射波束成形、阵列姿态调整和单元视轴控制；通过零强迫分析揭示信道强度增强与空间正交性改善的几何原理。

Result: 仿真验证联合方案在加权最大和速率上优于固定阵列、纯姿态调整及纯视轴转向基准，尤其在方向性元件与严格转向约束时增益显著。

Conclusion: 几何可重构天线技术可通过动态相位-增益信道整形显著提升多用户通信性能，为灵活天线系统设计提供新范式。

Abstract: This paper investigates geometry-reconfigurable transmission for multiuser communication systems enabled by a rotatable antenna array. In contrast to conventional fixed arrays, the proposed architecture jointly exploits array pose adjustment and element-level boresight steering, thereby reshaping both the array-induced phase responses and the direction-dependent channel gains. We formulate a weighted sum-rate maximization problem that jointly optimizes the transmit beamformers, array pose, and element boresights under practical visibility and steering constraints. To reveal the underlying design principles, we first provide a geometric interpretation via zero-forcing analysis, showing that the resulting rates stem from both channel-strength enhancement and spatial-separability improvement. Specifically, array-pose rotation improves inter-user channel orthogonality even with isotropic elements, whereas directional elements introduce a tradeoff between phase-based spatial separation and boresight-dependent gain alignment. Motivated by these insights, we develop an efficient optimization framework that jointly coordinates transmit beamforming, array-pose adaptation, and element-boresight steering to exploit the geometry-induced phase-and-gain channel-shaping capability. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed joint design outperforms fixed-array, pose-only, and boresight-only benchmarks, with larger gains achieved under more directive element patterns and tighter boresight-steering constraints.

</details>


### [282] [Robust Transmission Design for RIS-Assisted RSMA-SWIPT Systems With Movable Antennas Under Hardware Distortions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02384)
*Muhammad Asif,Asim Ihsan,Irfan Muhammad,Mohd Hamza Naim Shaikh,Syed Tariq Shah,Zhu Shoujin,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对存在CSI不确定性和硬件损伤的MA-RIS增强型多用户RSMA-SWIPT系统，提出鲁棒资源分配框架，通过联合优化天线位置、波束赋形、RIS反射系数等参数，有效提升系统鲁棒性和和速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MA系统中信道有效性依赖天线位置，导致CSI不确定性同时影响主动/被动波束赋形和天线位置优化；硬件损伤进一步造成SINR失真，使波束赋形、RIS控制、速率分配和功率分割等问题高度耦合，形成极具挑战的资源分配问题。

Method: 提出鲁棒资源分配框架，联合优化公共速率分配、发送波束赋形、RIS反射系数、功率分割比和MA位置；通过分解为主动波束赋形、RIS设计、功率分割和天线位置优化四个子问题，构建可处理的凸替代函数处理非凸目标函数和约束。

Result: 仿真实验证明所提框架在可达和速率、对CSI不确定性和硬件损伤的鲁棒性及收敛性能方面显著优于基准方案。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了MA-RIS增强型SWIPT系统中CSI不确定性和硬件损伤共同作用下的复杂资源分配问题，为相关系统设计提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: This paper investigates a robust transmission design for a multi-user rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA)-based simultaneous wireless information and power transfer (SWIPT) system empowered by movable antennas (MAs) and a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) under channel state information (CSI) uncertainty and residual hardware impairments (HIs). The effective channels in MAs-enabled systems depend on antenna positions, causing CSI uncertainty to affect not only active and passive beamforming but also antenna position optimization. Furthermore, residual HIs distort the effective SINRs, creating additional coupling among beamforming, RIS reflection control, common-rate allocation, power-splitting ratio optimization, and antenna position optimization. Consequently, the joint impact of CSI uncertainty and HIs leads to a highly coupled and challenging resource allocation problem. To address this challenge, we propose a robust resource allocation framework that jointly optimizes common-rate allocation, transmit beamforming, RIS reflection coefficients, power-splitting ratios, and MAs positions to maximize the achievable sum-rate while satisfying practical system constraints. To obtain an efficient solution, the original problem is decomposed into active beamforming, RIS reflection design, power-splitting ratio optimization, and MAs position optimization subproblems, where tractable convex surrogate functions are constructed to handle the non-convex objective and constraints. Simulation results verify the effectiveness of the proposed framework and demonstrate substantial improvements in achievable sum-rate, robustness against CSI uncertainty and hardware impairments, and convergence performance compared with benchmark schemes.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [283] [Fully Unsupervised Detection of Physical Contacts on Subsea Cables via State-of-Polarization Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01484)
*Agastya Raj,Alvaro Doval,Tian Tian,Steinar Bjørnstad,Marco Ruffini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种完全无监督的Fast-Slow DSVDD检测器，用于已部署海底电缆的连续偏振态监测。该方法在无需事件标签的情况下，成功将5个确认的拖网渔船接触事件在122,174个录音中排名前13位，并发现了额外的有证据支持的电缆接触事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海底电缆需要实时监测外部干扰事件（如拖网渔船接触），但这类事件标签获取困难。现有方法通常依赖有监督学习，需要大量标注数据，而实际部署中难以获得。因此，需要开发一种无监督方法，利用电缆自身状态信号（偏振态变化）进行异常检测。

Method: 采用Fast-Slow DSVDD（深度支持向量数据描述）检测器，结合快速和慢速时间尺度的特征建模，对连续采集的偏振态数据进行分析。该方法通过重构误差或距离度量识别异常模式，无需预标注的训练数据。

Result: 在122,174个实际录音数据集中，该检测器成功将5个已知拖网渔船接触事件排在异常分数最高的前13位，同时发现了额外的有证据支持的电缆接触事件，证明了其实际检测能力。

Conclusion: 该无监督方法有效实现了海底电缆接触事件的实时监测，无需事件标签即可准确识别异常，为实际部署的海底基础设施提供了可行的监控解决方案。

Abstract: We present a fully unsupervised Fast-Slow DSVDD detector for continuous State-of-Polarization monitoring on a deployed subsea cable. Trained without event labels, it ranks all five confirmed trawler contacts within the top 13 of 122,174 recordings and surfaces additional corroborated cable-contact events.

</details>


### [284] [Enabling Real-Time AI in O-RAN: Deploying andMeasuring AI Inside a Near-RT RIC xApp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01583)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Sampath Jayarathna,Safdar Hussain Bouk,Fahmida Afrin,C. Nicolas Barati,Neda Moghim,Valentina Nanou,Muhammad Enayetur Rahman,Sachin Shetty*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在O-RAN架构下实现了嵌入Near-RT RIC软件循环的AI推理xApp，使用轻量级逻辑回归和浅层MLP模型，在合成数据集上验证了网络状态分类的可行性，实测推理延迟满足10ms时延预算要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI已广泛用于RAN优化，但少有工作在实际测试床上演示嵌入Near-RT RIC软件循环的测量AI推理，缺乏对确定性执行和时序约束的验证。

Method: 基于OpenAirInterface和FlexRIC测试床实现AI使能的网络状态分类xApp，使用MAC、RLC、PDCP、GTP和UE计数特征构建合成数据集，将逻辑回归和浅层MLP导出为确定性C推理模块并编译到xApp二进制中，消除外部ML运行时依赖。

Result: 逻辑回归推理延迟1-5微秒，MLP为10-25微秒；端到端服务延迟低于4ms；六种监督模型准确率均在0.88-0.90范围；噪音消融、混淆矩阵分析和CDF时延特征表明两模型在95%以上执行中满足10ms Near-RT预算。

Conclusion: 轻量级AI可在Near-RT RIC时序约束内运行并保证确定性执行，为生产级部署奠定基础；同时开源RIC Workbench为复现测试床提供工具支持。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) architectures introduce programmable Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controllers (Near-RT RICs) that support closed-loop control through xApps at timescales from 10 ms to 1 s. Although AI has been widely studied for RAN optimization, fewer works demonstrate measured AI inference embedded directly within the Near-RT RIC software loop on a live testbed. This paper presents an AI-enabled network-state classification xApp implemented on an OpenAirInterface (OAI) and FlexRIC testbed. The xApp is trained and evaluated on a structured synthetic dataset that emulates cross-layer RAN states using MAC, RLC, PDCP, GTP, and UE-count features. The results validate embedding and execution feasibility rather than production-level generalization. Logistic regression and a shallow multilayer perceptron (MLP) are exported as deterministic C inference modules and compiled into the xApp binary, eliminating external machine-learning runtime dependencies. Measured inference latency is 1 to 5 microseconds for logistic regression and 10 to 25 microseconds for the MLP, while end-to-end service latency remains below 4 ms. A six-model comparison shows that supervised models achieve similar accuracy, ranging from 0.88 to 0.90, indicating that LR and MLP similarity reflects the proxy problem structure rather than limited model exploration. Noise ablation, confusion-matrix analysis, and CDF-based latency characterization show that both embedded models satisfy the 10 ms Near-RT budget for more than 95% of projected loop executions. These results demonstrate that lightweight AI can operate within Near-RT RIC timing constraints while preserving deterministic execution. We also release RIC Workbench, a lightweight orchestration dashboard for reproducing the testbed on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [285] [CSI Simulation: Why Additive Noise Fails and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01882)
*Aymen Bouferroum,Ildi Alla,Vincent Lenders,Valeria Loscri*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了现有CSI模拟方法中关于接收链线性增益不变的假设不成立，提出M_QTC模型通过分位数映射等技术显著提升模拟数据保真度，使下游任务性能接近真实数据水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSI模型训练依赖加性高斯白噪声(AWGN)模拟数据，其核心假设是天线到信道估计器间的接收链为线性且增益不变。然而该假设在真实场景中可能失效，导致模拟数据与实测数据存在显著分布差异，影响模型泛化能力。

Method: 提出测量校准模型M_QTC，包含三个关键技术：1) 基于分位数映射的子载波分布变换学习；2) 时序滤波处理；3) 基于Copula函数的跨子载波重排序机制。该模型直接建模真实射频链路中的非线性变换效应。

Result: 在4个室内环境的6类商用接收设备上测试表明：1) M_QTC将幅度误差降低8倍；2) 在四个互补维度上填补了89%的数据保真度差距；3) 基于M_QTC模拟数据训练的5类分类器在干扰检测任务中恢复93%的真实数据性能，而AWGN训练的分类器仅接近随机决策水平。

Conclusion: 证实自动增益控制机制破坏了CSI模拟的传统线性假设，所提M_QTC模型通过精确建模非线性变换过程，有效弥合了模拟与实测数据间的分布鸿沟，为依赖CSI模拟的应用提供了更可靠的数据生成方案。

Abstract: Channel State Information (CSI) has become a widely used wireless channel sensing modality for applications such as indoor localization, activity recognition, and respiration monitoring. Because collecting labeled data under every target condition is impractical, training CSI-based models often relies on simulated data produced by adding noise or perturbations to recorded channel estimates, most commonly additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN). This practice assumes that the receiver chain between the antenna and the channel estimator is linear and gain-invariant. We test this assumption empirically using RF jamming as a controlled perturbation on 6 commodity receivers across 2 indoor environments. The assumption does not hold. Automatic gain control compresses the channel estimate multiplicatively before digitization, producing amplitude distributions that no additive noise variance can reproduce.
